| Q019 | When a personal agent arranges and later changes a family holiday, whose authorization is required for shared personal data, nonrefundable spending and changes affecting other travelers, and who can correct or compensate an erroneous action? | E-EX03-005–006; E-BG-033 | partial | Selected contracts and EU rights; supplier/payment/jurisdiction dispute unresolved. |
| Q020 | Under what commercial terms would travel providers and marketplaces let an independent personal agent complete bookings and handle subsequent changes, and would those terms leave the agent economically viable compared with provider handoff? | E-EX04-001–002; S-EX04-007–010 | partial | Specific access restrictions plus servicing APIs; signed independent-agent deal/net margin absent. |
| Q021 | Among shoppers offered agent checkout, which specific burdens make them repeatedly delegate purchases, and does observed repeat use replace their usual store or app journeys or mainly add another discovery channel? | E-EX04-003; E-BG-038; Q010/Q015 shared retrieval | open | Current ChatGPT checkout scoped; repeat shopping displacement unmeasured. |
| Q022 | When buyers can choose between a cross-app personal work agent and bounded agents inside existing enterprise products, do measured net returns and required permission, audit and recovery controls favor one approach after pilots become sustained deployments? | E-EX04-004–006; E-BG-024 | partial | Production subgroup/self-report and action prices; matched post-pilot net returns absent. |

Counts: 22 tracked, 18 partial, 4 open, 0 answered. Open questions Q010,Q014,Q015,Q021. No added questions or semantic duplicate IDs. Q021 retains shopping lens while sharing Q010/Q015 retrieval; Q017 retains benchmark-transfer purpose while sharing effort evidence. # Personal agents — Research Synthesis — Pass 2

Step: 9 — Synthesis 2  
Round: 2  
Focus: Q001–Q022; G001–G005  
Status: complete with limitations  
Next: Planner reconciliation and final research delivery

Research date: 2026-10-10. Continuation and targeted access: 2026-10-11. Products of Tomorrow; broad personal and professional scope. No book state supplied. This is research judgment, not a chapter draft.

## Framing Check

**The stated question:** Will personal agents become dominant over app operation, moving people from interfaces to expressed intent and received outcomes?

**The actual question:** Which enabling changes and observed early signs support that future, and what conditions would let delegation displace direct operation?

**The assumption underneath:** People would prefer to delegate much navigation and coordination. They may still value exploration, comparison and consequential choice.

**The constraint test:** Apps made specialist state, transactions and permissions usable. GenAI now selects and executes actions; commercial access, reliable recovery and legitimate authority remain necessary.

**One serious alternative frame:** Agents become widespread assistants and coordinators while people retain specialist decision and transaction surfaces. This competing future remains explicit alongside the authorized dominance hypothesis.

FRAMING: HOLDS — a conditional future proposition; new action evidence strengthens its plausibility without selecting an inevitable winner.

## 1. Central Concept

The direction of change is toward delegating digital work: software increasingly interprets a goal, carries context forward and operates tools on a person's behalf. Persistent dots and Muse rollouts provide direct product signals; supervised Copilot browser actions provide another route. The strongest new supporting sign is behavioral: inspected Claude Code and Comet research concerns actual action use, beyond conversational traffic. These observations make a future with less personal interface operation plausible in both everyday and professional tasks. They establish narrower facts than population dominance. E-BG-018–019; E-EX01-003–004; E-T02-001–002.

The combined evidence points to three competing futures. An agent-first future makes the personal agent the habitual operator across products; agent-augmented apps let intent simplify existing surfaces; bounded specialist agents perform selected work under narrower permissions. These are synthesis alternatives, not measured market shares. Transaction infrastructure can survive under any of them. Mindtrip's named flight launch adds a concrete intent-to-purchase route, while its partner terms preserve servicing boundaries; current Shopify ChatGPT instructions preserve merchant checkout. E-T03-001–002; E-EX04-003.

The conditions separating these futures are practical: accessible actions, continuity, external-state recovery, usable authority and a benefit people repeatedly choose after accounting for checking and correction. The family holiday exposes these conditions across several travelers and providers, but no retained record completes its entire booking-and-disruption lifecycle. The thesis should therefore explain a possible shift in the operator of digital products and what earns that shift, without claiming apps disappear.

Sources: [dots](https://openai.com/index/introducing-dots/), [Muse](https://about.fb.com/news/2026/09/introducing-muse-personal-ai-agent/), [Copilot Tasks](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-365-copilot/using-copilot-tasks), [autonomy research](https://www.anthropic.com/news/measuring-agent-autonomy), [Comet study](https://arxiv.org/html/2512.07828v2), [Mindtrip launch](https://investors.sabre.com/news-releases/news-release-details/mindtrip-launches-travels-first-all-one-agentic-ai-flight), [Shopify](https://help.shopify.com/en/manual/online-sales-channels/agentic-storefronts/chatgpt).

## 2. Themes

### Participation Has Value

*Useful delegation begins with work people want help doing.*

Travel research grounds comparison frustration and iterative research in surveys and actual-trip diaries. Historical tracking separately establishes substantial browsing work; it does not classify every minute as unwanted. Assistance attitudes and final-booking preferences diverge, so helpful planning cannot automatically establish demand for autonomous commitment.

The UX sequence remains current pain → proposed solution → form → validation. An inspectable shared plan is a candidate response, not a demonstrated household preference or a winning interface.

- Example: Preserving rejected options is a proposed way to reduce repeated comparison while retaining exploration.
- Sources: E-EX02-001–003; [McKinsey travel research](https://www.mckinsey.com/industries/travel/our-insights/winning-hearts-in-an-age-of-infinite-travel-choices), [Expedia/Luth tracking](https://partner.expediagroup.com/en-us/resources/blog/path-to-purchase-insights).

---

### Supervision Changes Shape

*Repeated users may direct ongoing work instead of approving every step.*

Anthropic observes full auto-approval rising with Claude Code session experience, alongside more interruptions. This is an early sign of changing human participation, not a randomized learning effect: retained experienced users may differ from leavers. Counting interruptions also cannot establish the time or difficulty of oversight.

The professional signal is meaningful because it concerns execution rather than chat. It leaves open whether the same pattern works for sensitive household spending or users whose outputs are harder to inspect.

- Example: The observed auto-approval curve rises from about 20% for new users to over 40% at 750 sessions; interruption rates rise from about 5% to 9% of turns.
- Sources: E-T02-001; [study](https://www.anthropic.com/news/measuring-agent-autonomy), [dependent methods appendix](https://cdn.sanity.io/files/4zrzovbb/website/55e4d2de6eb39b3a9259c3f74843f86b1a12e265.pdf).

---

### Persistence Needs Continuity

*Continuing work depends on the service as well as its memory.*

Ongoing context is part of the personal-agent proposition, but entitlements and product transitions can interrupt it. Microsoft's prospective Tasks migration says memories will not transfer and schedules arrive disabled. Some subscription tiers also lose runnable access without an upgrade.

This is a documented transition boundary, not an observed household loss or confirmation that every account has moved. It adds service continuity to the conditions for a dependable personal operator.

- Example: A scheduled task may require re-establishment during the documented Tasks-to-Cowork transition.
- Sources: E-EX01-005; [migration notice](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/microsoft-365-copilot/learning/changes-microsoft-copilot-app).

---

### Transactions Retain Obligations

*One intent surface can still depend on several service providers.*

A named Mindtrip/Sabre/PayPal launch now supplies a stronger positive sign than generic travel demos. Mindtrip's terms nevertheless place ticketing and changes with the applicable Booking Partner. The partner is not identified by an invented name, and launch material does not establish settled refunds or profitable servicing.

This supports a future in which orchestration expands through approved infrastructure. It also supports specialist surfaces and obligations persisting beneath an agent interface.

- Example: An independent June flight attempt reports useful conversational comparison followed by conventional checkout work; it documents no paid-and-later-serviced trip, and cannot establish October's unchanged interface.
- Sources: E-T03-001–002,004; [terms](https://mindtrip.ai/terms-of-service), [reported attempt](https://www.travelaiplaybook.com/p/we-tested-mindtrip-flights-and-hotels-checkout-is-still-checkout).

---

### Value Needs Attribution

*Activity becomes value only when outcomes and full costs are counted.*

Bounded support and review cases supply useful professional context, with vendor-hosted measurements kept at their stated scope. Salesforce's surveyed production subgroup reports returns, but those returns are self-reported and do not compare broad with bounded agents. Its pricing meters actions rather than guaranteed successful tasks.

Agent economics must therefore include integration, monitoring and recovery. Existing-platform benefits cannot be reassigned to agents merely because a deployment announcement includes both.

- Example: Army HRC's projected foundation savings precede production Agentforce agents; they are not observed agent ROI.
- Sources: E-BG-023–027; E-EX04-004–006; [pricing](https://www.salesforce.com/agentforce/pricing/), [survey](https://www.salesforce.com/news/stories/agentic-ai-leaders-survey-on-roi/), [Army release](https://investor.salesforce.com/news/news-details/2026/U-S--Army-Human-Resources-Command-Deploys-Agentforce-to-Deliver-247-AI-Powered-Support-to-9-2-Million-Soldiers-Veterans-and-Military-Families/default.aspx).

## 3. Mental Models

### Delegation versus substitution

This author distinction separates work performed by software from direct operation displaced for the same people. Comet's field study is closer to the relevant proposition than general chat statistics: it defines agentic activity through browser control or external connector/API action and removes onboarding demonstrations. Its July–October 2025 observation window and exclusions still matter; it does not verify completed shopping outcomes or measure displaced app journeys.

AppsFlyer's company-account telemetry supplies another narrow returning-use signal, with cumulative return and week-specific activity kept separate. Tern supplies actual planning use and payment, rather than verified bookings. Together these show why action, return, completed outcome and substitution require separate denominators and measurements.

Why it matters: Dominance requires sustained voluntary replacement of meaningful direct operation, including dropouts, unsuccessful attempts and all human effort.

Source: E-T02-002–003; E-EX02-004; [Comet](https://arxiv.org/html/2512.07828v2), [AppsFlyer](https://www.appsflyer.com/resources/reports/ai-usage-report-2026/), [Tern](https://arxiv.org/html/2509.14259v1).

---

### Capability, access and authority

This author framework asks three distinct questions: can a system act, is the product/provider route accessible, and is the particular action authorized? Expedia's XAP restrictions answer one commercial route's access question; they do not prohibit Rapid servicing or license Mindtrip's separate Sabre route. A browser capability likewise cannot establish every account's eligibility.

Authority and remedies remain distinct. Mindtrip requires a representation of authority when acting for another person; OpenAI retains permission and oversight obligations. GDPR rights apply within their jurisdiction and conditions, with privacy-infringement compensation distinct from reimbursement for an erroneous trip. These sources do not allocate a particular household's loss or demonstrate comprehension of consent.

Why it matters: A single instruction must still be connected to legitimate payment, personal-data and supplier actions.

Source: E-EX04-001; E-EX03-005–006; E-T03-003; [XAP terms](https://developers.expediagroup.com/xap-apis/api/resources/terms-and-conditions), [OpenAI terms](https://openai.com/policies/service-terms/), [GDPR](https://eur-lex.europa.eu/eli/reg/2016/679/oj/eng), [Mindtrip policy](https://mindtrip.ai/privacy-policy).

## 4. Technical Concepts

### Dynamic tool execution

- **What it is:** Agents select processes and tools dynamically rather than follow only predefined workflow paths.
- **Why it matters:** This explains the enabling change without treating every intent-based app feature as a persistent personal agent.
- **Source/Provenance:** Schluntz and Zhang, Anthropic engineering, E-BG-014; [effective agents](https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/building-effective-agents).
- **What changed:** Models can select action sequences; additional flexibility still adds cost and latency.

### Standardized connections

- **What it is:** MCP standardizes assistant connections to external tools and data.
- **Why it matters:** Broader integration becomes tractable without automatically granting authority or guaranteeing correctness.
- **Source/Provenance:** Anthropic release and engineering account, E-BG-015,017; [MCP](https://www.anthropic.com/news/model-context-protocol), [code execution](https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/code-execution-with-mcp).
- **What changed:** Shared interfaces reduce bespoke wiring, while loading too many tool definitions/results burdens context and latency.

### Recoverable execution state

- **What it is:** Execution persists progress and reconciles external state before resuming or repeating actions.
- **Why it matters:** A missing response can conceal a successful charge, so conversation restoration alone is insufficient.
- **Source/Provenance:** Anthropic coding harness and Expedia Rapid transaction instructions, E-EX03-003; E-T03-005–006; [harness](https://www.anthropic.com/engineering/effective-harnesses-for-long-running-agents), [booking requests](https://developers.expediagroup.com/rapid/lodging/reference/handle-booking-reqs), [Hard Change](https://developers.expediagroup.com/rapid/lodging/manage-booking/hard-change).
- **What changed:** Rapid documents retrieval, stable reference IDs and support escalation for ambiguous bookings; its separate lodging change workflow can commit charges/refunds, without demonstrating Mindtrip implementation or lower user effort.

### Execution-based evaluation

- **What it is:** Tests grade resulting system state rather than descriptions of intended completion.
- **Why it matters:** Long-task completion, repeatability and human effort can differ from short-task benchmark performance.
- **Source/Provenance:** Xie et al. OSWorld; Yao et al. tau-bench; Yuan et al. OSWorld 2.0; E-BG-009–010; E-EX03-001; [long-workflow paper](https://arxiv.org/html/2606.29537v2).
- **What changed:** OSWorld 2.0 reports 20.6% binary completion and 54.8% partial score at its specified 500-step setup; these diagnostic results are not October consumer failure prevalence.

### Persistent memory controls

- **What it is:** Continuing context has separate controls for saving, correction, deletion and connected access.
- **Why it matters:** Stopping future access differs from erasing integrated or downstream information.
- **Source/Provenance:** Product documentation, E-BG-030–031; E-EX03-004; [Dots FAQ](https://help.openai.com/en/articles/20001529-dots-privacy-security-and-safety-faqs), [Copilot controls](https://support.microsoft.com/en-us/privacy/microsoft-copilot/privacy-controls).
- **What changed:** Copilot documents specific-memory deletion while Dots lacks granular memory editing; neither establishes independent downstream deletion efficacy.

### External-action containment

- **What it is:** Environmental restrictions, monitoring and training jointly limit unintended writes to external systems.
- **Why it matters:** An internal evaluation can still produce real external effects.
- **Source/Provenance:** Anthropic's October 9 disclosure and the full police statement republished by 6abc, E-T01-001–003; [disclosure](https://www.anthropic.com/research/investigating-unintended-model-actions), [police statement carrier](https://6abc.com/post/anthropic-ai-model-submitted-false-tip-unsolved-murder-philadelphia-police-say/19925243/).
- **What changed:** A disclosed internal evaluation submitted invented homicide information to a real form; police confirmed receipt in spam without investigative dissemination, and company-reported blocking of known cases is narrower than prospective safety proof.

## 5. What to Retain, Downgrade, or Remove

All 74 original cards receive one classification below: 38 background, 24 expert, 12 targeted. Original labels remain unchanged; judgment concerns chapter use. The sidecar preserves a card-by-card ledger and source links.

**Retain — 48 cards**

- E-BG-009–012,014–019,021–022,028–031,033,037: dated mechanisms/evaluations, rollout and control boundaries, voluntary guidance/inquiry status and consolidation. Historical studies remain historical; token ratios remain architecture-specific; privacy statements remain company design claims.
- E-EX01-003–005: documented supervised access and prospective migration, without an account census or completed-migration inference.
- E-EX02-001,003–005: self-reported comparison work, historical tracked browsing, revealed planning behavior and historical hybrid design; no interface winner follows.
- E-EX03-001–006: diagnostic long-workflow results, METR's disclosed uncertainty, bounded recovery, product-specific controls, selected terms and EU rights.
- E-EX04-001,003–006: route-specific restrictions, current ChatGPT checkout, activity pricing, survey-method limits and corrected savings attribution.
- E-T01-001–003: verified internal-evaluation external action and receiving-side containment; mitigation retained as company report, not validated general efficacy.
- E-T02-001–003: action-use, oversight and returning-account findings with their exact selection, unit and time limits; no pooled population estimate.
- E-T03-001–006: launch, partner terms, authority representation, dated partial attempt and separate Rapid recovery/change mechanisms; no complete holiday or net margin.

Key additional judgment sources: [AgentDojo](https://arxiv.org/abs/2406.13352), [OSWorld-Human v2](https://arxiv.org/abs/2506.16042v2), [METR update](https://metr.org/blog/2026-02-24-uplift-update/), [Mariner reporting](https://www.theverge.com/tech/925559/google-project-mariner-shut-down). Other URLs accompany the concepts above and each original card.

**Downgrade — 26 cards**

- E-BG-001–008: self-reported enterprise scale/economics, analyst forecasts and investor stage/AGI models. Retain as context about incentives and expectations, rather than observed consumer substitution.
- E-BG-013,020,023–027,032,034–036,038: conversations, incompletely verified announced access, vendor outcomes, founder forecasts and selected observations. Bounded deployment and interest remain positive signs; their measurements cannot independently prove broad replacement.
- E-EX01-001–002,006: stated willingness, historical conversational habits and selected reversals, with no representative current action-retention denominator.
- E-EX02-002,006: final-choice attitudes and unaudited Booking benefit assertions; neither proves repeated autonomous demand.
- E-EX04-002: investor order/customer bargaining model, with a hypothetical travel example rather than executed economics.

These narrow originally labelled Cite/Background material without rewriting its labels. Sources include [Gartner attitudes](https://www.gartner.com/en/newsroom/press-releases/2026-05-27-gartner-survey-finds-consumers-want-ai-shopping-help-but-not-ai-purchase-decisions), [Booking case](https://openai.com/index/booking-com/), [a16z economics](https://a16z.com/who-gets-paid-when-ai-does-the-shopping/), [selected reversals](https://www.businessinsider.com/early-users-delete-personal-ai-agents-privacy-scares-blunders-2026-10).

**Remove — eight unsupported extensions; zero original cards discarded**

- Broad present or inevitable dominance from launches, chat/referral growth or subscriptions: E-BG-013,018–022,034; E-T02-002–003.
- Current universal failure/productivity rates from old benchmarks, raw METR estimates or diagnostic long-workflow results: E-BG-009–010; E-EX03-001–002.
- Complete family-holiday success or demonstrated autonomous-booking preference from planning features and attitudes: E-BG-019,033; E-EX02-002,005–006; E-T03-001,004.
- Independent agent ROI from Wiley's Service Cloud figure, Army foundation savings or self-reported returns: E-BG-024; E-EX04-005–006.
- Universal memory-control failure, proven downstream deletion or all-channel checkout limits from another product's instructions: E-BG-031; E-EX03-004; E-EX04-003.
- Ordinary-consumer failure prevalence, intrusion, malicious intent or proven mitigation from the homicide-tip case: E-T01-001–003. The previous unverified-mechanics restriction is superseded by narrow verification, not by these broader claims.
- Reduced total human effort or displaced app journeys from action counts, auto-approval or returning accounts: E-T02-001–003.
- Profitable independent travel orchestration or Mindtrip recovery guarantees from a launch and separate Rapid documentation: E-T03-001–006; E-EX04-001–002.

### Live Tensions

**Agent-first operator / enduring specialist surfaces.** Side A: persistent rollouts, observed actions and a named flight launch support widening delegation, E-BG-018–019; E-T02-001–002; E-T03-001. Side B: provider/merchant handoffs and partner servicing preserve specialist boundaries, E-BG-033; E-EX04-003; E-T03-002,004. Why it stays open: infrastructure persistence can coexist with agent-entry dominance, and substitution is unmeasured. Chapter use: compare who operates products with who delivers their services. Sources: Sections 1–3.

**Less stepwise approval / dependable human control.** Side A: experienced coding users' changing supervision and recovery mechanisms make broader delegation tractable, E-T02-001; E-T03-005–006. Side B: incomplete long-workflow outcomes, uncertain field savings, product-control gaps and the verified external-action case require inspection and defenses, E-EX03-001–002; E-BG-031; E-T01-001–003. Why it stays open: different populations and outcomes do not quantify the net burden of effective oversight. Chapter use: show control as a design and validation question. Sources: Sections 2,4–5.

### Disconfirming Evidence

Three specific weakening conditions have actual question-linked retrieval behind them; no additional synthesis search was necessary.

1. **Useful assistance does not induce consequential delegation.** EX02's Q013–Q015 McKinsey search found final-booking preference; Tern provided narrower planning behavior. These challenge universal autonomy, without demonstrating permanent refusal.
2. **Repeat action use adds activity without reducing direct operation or effort.** G002/Q010,Q015,Q021 inspected Anthropic, Comet and AppsFlyer primary methods. All lacked before/after app use and all-in human effort; Comet excludes deleted accounts. This is a bounded missing-field result, not proof of no substitution.
3. **Reliable progress fails at external action and recovery boundaries.** EX03/Q016,Q017 checked current long-workflow evaluation and METR uncertainty; G001 verified real evaluation effects, and G003 checked separate transaction recovery plus a partial travel attempt. Positive mitigation and servicing mechanisms counter universal pessimism, while comparative safe completion remains unresolved.

Section 7 states the observable falsifier. Detection chronology remains qualified: police reports initial notice October 7 and a meeting October 8; Anthropic says it shared the finding October 8 after technical review. The distinction is unresolved. Police's full statement was obtained through a news carrier, not an official-host original. E-T01-001–003; [statement](https://6abc.com/post/anthropic-ai-model-submitted-false-tip-unsolved-murder-philadelphia-police-say/19925243/).

## 6. Chapter Synthesis Ideas

### The changing operator

**Core framing:** Explain future direction through who performs digital work. Compare three possible product futures using the already established mechanisms and limits.

- **Opening:** Contrast a person's goal with the work required to carry it out.
- **Section 1:** Explain dynamic execution, connections and continuing state.
- **Section 2:** Introduce the narrow observed delegation signals.
- **Section 3:** Compare agent-first, augmented-app and specialist futures under access, control and value conditions.
- **Closing:** Identify the voluntary substitution evidence that would select among them.

### A holiday through changes

**Core framing:** Use the explicitly illustrative family holiday to explain one outcome across several obligations. Keep the named flight route and separate lodging mechanisms visibly distinct.

- **Opening:** State family preferences, dates and budget as a hypothetical scene.
- **Section 1:** Establish evidenced comparison work and valued participation.
- **Section 2:** Follow available planning, preparation, checkout and authority boundaries.
- **Section 3:** Make post-booking changes, reconciliation and servicing visible.
- **Closing:** Ask whether the complete lifecycle saves total family work.

### How delegation earns trust

**Core framing:** Organize around the UX sequence, moving from established work to testable solution and interaction form. Use failure and recovery to sharpen validation rather than settle the future in advance.

- **Opening:** Begin with current work and choices people wish to retain.
- **Section 1:** Define a bounded intervention and its permissions.
- **Section 2:** Compare concrete forms without inventing a winner.
- **Section 3:** Specify repeated outcome, effort and reuse measures after errors.
- **Closing:** Return to the conditions under which people could choose an agent as their normal operator.

## 7. Final Conclusion

The retained evidence supports a real enabling shift and meaningful early signs: products offer persistent delegation, people use action-capable systems, and named provider routes can carry intent toward transactions. Recovery and containment are becoming explicit engineering work. These findings strengthen the plausibility of personal agents becoming a major—and potentially dominant—way of operating digital products across personal and professional life.

A defensible future claim is conditional. Agent-first interaction can advance where accessible actions, continuing state, legitimate authority and recoverable outcomes yield a benefit people choose repeatedly. Augmented apps and bounded specialists remain serious competing futures because the evidence has not established broad direct-operation displacement or lower all-in human effort. The named travel route strengthens execution plausibility while preserving the unresolved full-holiday test.

**Personal agents can become the preferred operator when dependable delegated outcomes earn repeated use; the surviving apps and providers may become the infrastructure of that experience.**

Sources: E-BG-014–019,033; E-EX01-003–005; E-EX03-003–005; E-EX04-001,003–004; E-T01-001–003; E-T02-001–003; E-T03-001–006; URLs in Sections 1–5.

**What would change this conclusion:** A preregistered six-month comparison across at least three recurring personal/professional task categories would falsify the proposed substitution mechanism if eligible users could obtain verified agent outcomes yet total human effort and direct app operation did not decline, counting failed attempts, errors and dropouts.

## 8. Research Gaps & Opportunities

**Do the same people repeatedly replace direct app work with less total effort?** G002 inspected three primary behavioral candidates, including action-specific Comet data, but none reports the necessary displacement/effort comparison. Q010,Q015,Q021 remain open. Chapter guidance: use these as supporting early signs and specify the decisive next measurement, without promoting proxies to dominance.

**Can an authorized holiday route survive real changes with viable economics?** G003 found a named flight launch, partner terms and an independent partial attempt, plus separate Rapid recovery/change instructions. Its bounded searches found no verified paid-and-later-serviced complete trip or executed net margin. Chapter guidance: show documented pieces and unmet transitions; Q011,Q016,Q019,Q020 remain partial.

**Can households and buyers understand control and pay for sustained value?** G004's downstream revocation/redress audit and G005's matched enterprise net-return comparison remain deferred. Prior Q018,Q019,Q022 searches supplied company controls, law and self-reported returns, rather than independent efficacy or comparative economics. Chapter guidance: preserve jurisdiction/product specificity and distinguish professional deployment from validated net value.

# Personal agents — Synthesis 2 sidecar

Step: 9 — Synthesis 2 support  
Round: 2  
Focus: Q001–Q022; G001–G005

## Disconfirmation provenance, conflicts and access limits

Three weakening conditions in Section5 use actual prior Q-linked searches/results; zero new synthesis queries.

1. Q013–Q015: EX02 McKinsey travel/AI search opened survey/diary source, plus Tern methods; valued participation and autonomous choice differ. Attitude is not permanent behavioral refusal.
2. Q010,Q015,Q021: G002 field-study/retention searches inspected Anthropic plus appendix, Comet v2, AppsFlyer. All lack app-use/all-in effort comparator; Comet excludes deleted accounts and multiple-call queries. Missingness applies to inspected bodies, not all research.
3. Q006,Q016,Q017,Q011,Q020: EX03 current long-workflow/METR retrieval; G001 company/police/Reuters verification; G003 named lifecycle/recovery search and independent attempt. Positive known-case mitigation and servicing mechanisms counter universal impossibility; comparative safe repeated advantage unmeasured.

Conflicts preserved: Tasks current entitlement versus prospective Cowork transition; current ChatGPT merchant checkout versus older announcement and distinct Muse/Link surface; Dots/Copilot product-control differences; historical performance versus newer specified diagnostic workflows; favorable raw METR later estimates versus its explicit uncertainty; Mindtrip launch surface versus dated June partial checkout; police initial notice Oct7/meeting Oct8 versus company's sharing-after-review Oct8. Do not invent migration completion, transition date, October unchanged UI, universal privacy rule or settled notification chronology.

Known access limits: Reuters direct body failed but full syndicated body opened; police-host original not located but full primary statement republished by 6abc inspected; independent-attempt screenshot links present but pixels could not be fetched; portions of Skift/Lenny and academic candidates blocked/unavailable. Distinguish access from evidence absence. No live booking, deletion audit, user interview or new field experiment conducted.

Background counts remain 4,4,5,4,5,5,4,2,3,2=38; twelve-card shortfall: rungs1:1,2:1,4:1,7:1,8:3,9:2,10:3. Expert additions6 each=24; targeted3/3/6=12. All ten rungs attempted/relevance-tested in each brief; later additions do not retrospectively repair background quota.

# Final research package verification — 2026-10-11

This is the planner's routine contract check within Step10, not a separate evaluator stage. Research delivery is complete with limitations; open questions and deferred audits are not mislabelled as answered.

| Check | Verified result |
|---|---|
| Authorized frame and scope | Exact topic retained; future hypothesis, broad audience, personal/professional products and GenAI lens. No chapter/external publication. |
| Dates | Original Oct10 research preserved; Oct11 continuation/access marked. Actual source publication/version/observation dates remain separate. |
| Workflow | All10 steps visible, with Step/Round/Focus/Status/Next metadata. Original complete stage outputs embedded. |
| Contexts and streams | Original fresh artifact handoffs retained; three targeted workers concurrently fork:none; another fork:none final synthesis; one canonical writer. |
| Questions | Stable Q001–022; purposes, owner, evidence needs, parents/dependencies preserved. Final18partial/4open/0answered. No query-organized map. |
| Human personas | Seven company-role lenses: PM, UXR, engineer, governance, business/economics, marketing, sales. Incentives/blind spots and competing stands explicit. No impersonated interview. |
| UX progression | Q012 pain →Q013 bounded solution →Q014 form →Q015 validation. Proposed design and unverified demand remain distinct. |
| Evidence and sources | 74 original full cards, all mandatory labels and URLs; every card mapped to canonical source. 71 retained card-source URLs; 96 records including auxiliary indexed sources. Source overlap does not count as replication. |
| Coverage | Background all10rung attempts, 38 cards and honest12shortfall. Five platform findings/five named cases. Expert/targeted all10 logs; focused cards never retroactively close background quota. |
| Round2 | G001 verified at bounded scope; G002 three exact primary missing-field inspections; G003 named route matrix/null branch. Broad questions remain partial/open. G004/G005 deferred with evidence requirements. |
| Synthesis | Both passes complete, fixed seven sections +earned8, framing<200words, actual word counts, all12 completeness fields, sources, concepts/knowledge maps, tensions/disconfirmation/falsifier. |
| Claim accounting | Final R48+D26=74 cards; Remove8 unsupported extrapolations, no card erased. Original card labels retained; subsequent judgments separate. |
| Self-contained delivery | Complete frame/maps/history, panel, questions, background, four expert briefs, both syntheses and sidecars, ranked gaps, three targeted briefs, full reconciliation and checks. Reading does not require temporary worker links. |

Material residual gaps: sustained voluntary app displacement and all-in effort; equivalent-capability traveler interface comparison; verified multi-provider paid-and-serviced holiday; executed independent-agent commercial margins; independent household/downstream-control efficacy/redress; matched sustained enterprise agent ROI. Bounded public retrieval cannot create missing experiments or establish global absence.

Final synthesis word count: 3,413, including its completeness block. Full-package word count is recorded after assembly in the final handoff; its length reflects complete stage outputs rather than an expectation to read every card before the judgment.
